对应解读：对应“建议重点查看的图表”第4项，以及“关键图表逐图导读”图4：不同相位的二维密度分布和速度场。
入选理由：该图直接展示四个相位中线驱动盘风的二维密度结构和速度矢量，是理解风基区、加速区和高速外流区如何随辐射周期移动的关键图。它能把Fig01中的时间滞后转化为空间上的风结构响应，比单纯时间曲线更能说明UFO可见性为何随相位变化。
来源与置信度：\(arxiv_source\)；high；\(arxiv_source\) / pasj02.tex:figure[3]

Fig06

图注：Radial profiles of the radial velocity (panel a), ionization parameter (panel b), and density (panel c) along representative viewing angles at which gas satisfying the UFO criterion is detected in the fiducial model. Panel d shows the column density of gas with velocities higher than $10^4\ {\rm km\ s^{-1}}$ and ionization parameters in the range $\log\xi = 2.5$--$5.5$. The red, blue, and green curves correspond to phase 3 at a viewing angle of $52^\circ$, phase 2 at a viewing angle of $73^\circ$, and phase 4 at a viewing angle of $52^\circ$, respectively. The gray regions in panels a, b, and d indicate where the corresponding physical quantities fall outside the ranges required to satisfy the UFO criterion. Alt text: Radial profiles of radial velocity, ionization parameter, density, and column density of gas with velocities higher than $10^4\ {\rm km\ s^{-1}}$ and ionization parameters in the range $\log\xi = 2.5$--$5.5$ at representative viewing angles in the fiducial model. The colored curves represent different phases and viewing angles. Gray regions indicate values outside the UFO criterion.
对应解读：对应“建议重点查看的图表”第5项和“模型拟合”段落中提到的按视角预测v、NH、xi和探测状态；也对应“关键图表逐图导读”关于UFO判据的诊断。
入选理由：该图沿代表性视角给出径向速度、电离参数、密度以及满足速度和电离条件气体的柱密度，并用灰区标出不满足UFO判据的范围。它最直接支撑哪些视角和相位真正会被观测为UFO，能够区分速度、电离和柱密度三个条件中哪个限制了探测。
来源与置信度：\(arxiv_source\)；high；\(arxiv_source\) / pasj02.tex:figure[6]

图注：Polar angle distributions of the density and the regions satisfying the UFO criterion (panel a), and the radial velocity (panel b) for the fiducial model. The black, blue, red, and green lines represent the results for phases 1, 2, 3, and 4, respectively. Alt text: Two line graphs labeled a and b showing angular profiles of density and radial velocity at four phases of the cycle. Each graph plots the quantities as functions of the polar angle, with four lines corresponding to the different phases.
对应解读：对应“建议重点查看的图表”第5项，以及“关键图表逐图导读”图5：倾角/角向分布与UFO可见性。
入选理由：该图给出极角方向上的密度、UFO判据区域和径向速度，可直接说明满足UFO条件的视角范围如何在不同相位改变。它支撑日报中“同一光度下不同视角可见性不同”和覆盖因子变化来自角向结构变化的解读。
来源与置信度：\(arxiv_source\)；high；\(arxiv_source\) / pasj02.tex:figure[4]

Fig05

图注：Two-dimensional distributions of the ionization parameter in the fiducial model. Yellow, pink, and cyan indicate regions with $\log\xi > 5.5$, $\log\xi = 2.5$--$5.5$, and $\log\xi < 2.5$, respectively. The regions where the gas radial velocity satisfies $v_r > 10^4\ {\rm km\ s^{-1}}$ are enclosed by shaded black contours. Alt text: Two-dimensional ionization-parameter distributions in the fiducial model, with colors indicating three $\log \xi$ ranges and shaded black contours marking gas with $v_r > 10^4\ {\rm km\ s^{-1}}$.
对应解读：对应“建议重点查看的图表”第4、5项，以及“关键图表逐图导读”中关于电离参数和高速但不过度电离区域的讨论。
入选理由：该图用二维颜色图显示电离参数分布，并用轮廓标出vr>10^4 km/s的高速区域，可判断高速气体是否处于可产生UFO吸收的合适电离范围。它补充Fig03的密度/速度快照，避免把所有高速低密度或过电离气体误认为可探测UFO。
来源与置信度：\(arxiv_source\)；high；\(arxiv_source\) / pasj02.tex:figure[5]

图注：Time evolution of the mass accretion rate onto the black hole (panel a), the disk luminosity (panel b), the mass outflow rate of the disk wind at $1500r_{\mathrm{s}}$ (panel c), and the covering factor of gas satisfying the UFO criterion (panel d). The black solid line represents the fiducial model, the black dashed line the Mdot3.8 model, the black dash-dotted line the Mdot0.56 model, and the red solid line the Alpha0.06 model. Alt text: Multi-panel figure with four line graphs showing, from panels a to d, the time evolution of the accretion rate, disk luminosity, mass outflow rate, and covering factor. 入选理由：该图比较基准模型与不同供给率、黏滞参数模型下的吸积率、光度、质量外流率和UFO覆盖因子时间演化，可检验延迟响应和覆盖因子峰值是否只出现在单一参数选择中。它对评估模型稳健性和未来参数网格拟合很重要。
来源与置信度：\(arxiv_source\)；high；\(arxiv_source\) / pasj02.tex:figure[7]

强相关工作

这项工作与几条文献线直接相连。第一条是标准薄盘和辐射压不稳定性，从 Shakura-Sunyaev 薄盘、Lightman-Eardley 不稳定到后来的极限环模型，核心问题是辐射压主导区是否真的不稳定，以及这种不稳定在 AGN 中的可观测时间尺度。建议检索关键词包括 “radiation pressure instability AGN accretion disk limit cycle”、 “thermal viscous instability slim disk” 和 “changing-look AGN disk instability”。

第二条是 AGN 线驱动盘风和 UFO 观测。Proga、Stone、Kallman 等人的二维线驱动风模拟奠定了基础，后续工作不断加入 X 射线屏蔽、电离平衡和谱合成。观测方面可检索 “AGN ultra-fast outflows variability”、 “Fe XXV Fe XXVI absorption UFO” 和 “Tombesi AGN UFO”。本文与这些工作的关系是把静态或准稳态风推进到由吸积盘极限环驱动的时间依赖风。

也存在潜在张力或替代解释。UFO 间歇性可以来自磁驱动风的团块结构、X 射线连续谱形状变化、局部遮蔽云经过视线、谱线显著性不足或仪器交叉定标问题，而不一定需要辐射压不稳定。要区分这些解释，需要同一源的长期多波段光变、重复高分辨率 X 射线谱和能追踪 \(N_{\rm H}\)、\(\xi\)、速度与光度相位关系的统一建模。
